Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The nuclear_physics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 465 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 877 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the nuclear_physics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^465 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 877-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
